\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.11299pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 10.2778pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip-1.88701pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to28.5556pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 1.94257pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Emp}$}}\vskip-1.88701pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\top:{\Rightarrow}$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 11.73315pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 18.18242pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.1111pt\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 11.73315pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to49.38878pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\top,\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 8.02965pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.4583pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to60.91661pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\bot}\mathrm{L}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\bot:\bot,\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.58035pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\bot,\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 8.02965pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 20.69438pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to49.38878pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\bot}\mathrm{L}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\bot,\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.25186pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.72224pt\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.75052pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to65.3738pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\bot}\mathrm{R}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\Gamma\Rightarrow\bot,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.1915pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\bot,\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.77965pt\hbox{\vbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w0,\mathrm{Inv}_{{\bot}\mathrm{R}}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 11.49725pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 11.49725pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to53.84596pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 11.49725pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.15187pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.23604pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{0}:\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}\hbox{\hskip 14.45377pt}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.95825pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w1$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{1}:\Gamma\Rightarrow\phi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-1.07036pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to143.69888pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 3.07034pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\to}\mathrm{L}$}}\vskip-1.07034pt\hbox{\hskip 21.5741pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{0}\vee\kappa_{1}:\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.2053pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\phi\to\psi,\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\\
\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.8352pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w0,\mathrm{Inv}_{{\to}\mathrm{L}_{0}}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 12.8798pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{0},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\hbox{\hskip 14.45377pt}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w1,\mathrm{Inv}_{{\to}\mathrm{L}_{1}}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 12.8798pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{1},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\vskip-0.9148pt\hbox{\hskip 12.8798pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to144.15295pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.91478pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\vee}\mathrm{L}$}}\vskip-0.91478pt\hbox{\hskip 49.34756pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{0}\vee\kappa^{*}_{1},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.15187pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 3.05551pt\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.43106pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow\psi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-1.07036pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to83.4014pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 3.07034pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\to}\mathrm{R}$}}\vskip-1.07034pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\Gamma\Rightarrow\phi\to\psi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.2053pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\phi\to\psi,\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.77965pt\hbox{\vbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w0,\mathrm{Inv}_{{\to}\mathrm{R}}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 10.42087pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 10.42087pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to53.84596pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 10.42087pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

Finally, assume
R
is
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{+})
.
Then
w
is of shape

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}\\
\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow\end{matrix}\right]_{i\leq 1}

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}\\
\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{i}\Rightarrow\phi\end{matrix}\right]_{i\leq 1,\phi\in\Theta_{i}}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{+}

\kappa_{w}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta

where

\kappa^{*}_{w}:=\bigwedge_{i\leq 1}\left(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*}\wedge\bigwedge_{\phi\in\Theta_{i}}\mathop{\text{\scalebox{1.1}{$\meddiamond$}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*}\right)\wedge\bigwedge(\Gamma\cap V_{+})\wedge\bigwedge\neg(\Delta\cap V_{-})

We define
\beta(w,\pi)
by case analysis on the shape of
\pi
.

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
(\mathrm{ax})
.
There are 4 cases depending on where the repeated propositional variable is located.

–

Assume
p\in\Gamma
and
p\in\Delta
.
This case is impossble, as
w
is annotated with
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{+})
implies that
\Gamma\cap\Delta=\varnothing
.

–

Assume
p\in\Gamma
and
p\in\Lambda
.
Since
p\in\Lambda
and
\mathrm{Voc}_{+}(\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda)\subseteq V_{+}
we have that
p\in V_{+}
.
Then, as
p\in\Gamma\cap V_{+}
and
p\not\in\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}
, we obtain that
p
is a conjunct of
\kappa^{*}_{w}
.
The desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

p,\Xi\Rightarrow p,\Lambda^{\prime}

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow p,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=p,\Lambda^{\prime}

–

Assume
p\in\Xi
and
p\in\Delta
.
Since
p\in\Xi
and
\mathrm{Voc}_{-}(\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda)\subseteq V_{-}
we have that
p\in V_{-}
.
Then, as
p\in\Delta\cap V_{-}
and
p\not\in\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}
, we obtain that
\neg p
is a conjunct of
\kappa^{*}_{w}
.
The desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

p,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow p,\Lambda

{\neg}\mathrm{L}

\neg p,p,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},p,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Lambda

where
\Xi=p,\Xi^{\prime}

–

Assume
p\in\Xi
and
p\in\Lambda
.
Then the desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

\kappa^{*}_{w},p,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow p,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Xi=p,\Xi^{\prime}
and
\Lambda=p,\Lambda^{\prime}
.

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\bot}\mathrm{L})
.
Since
\bot
is not in
\Gamma
(which consists in propositional variables only), then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

{\bot}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\bot,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Xi=\bot,\Xi^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

{\bot}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\bot,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\bot}\mathrm{R})
.
Since
\bot
is not in
\Delta
(which consists in propositional variables only), then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\bot}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\bot,\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda^{\prime}

{\bot}\mathrm{R}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\bot,\Xi\Rightarrow\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{L})
.
Since
\Gamma
consists of propositional variables only, we have that the principal formula of
({\to}\mathrm{L})
must be at
\Xi
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\phi,\Lambda

\pi_{1}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Xi=\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\phi,\Lambda

\beta(w,\pi_{1})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{R})
.
Since
\Delta
consists of propositional variables only, we have that the principal formula of
({\to}\mathrm{R})
must be at
\Lambda
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\phi,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\to}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\phi\to\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=\phi\to\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\phi,\Xi\Rightarrow\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\phi\to\psi,\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4}}_{i})
and the principal formula
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi
is in
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Theta_{i}
.
Then
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{\prime}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{\prime}_{\overline{i}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow\phi

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Sigma^{\prime}_{i}\subseteq\Sigma_{i}
,
\Sigma^{\prime}_{\overline{i}}\subseteq\Sigma_{\overline{i}}
and
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\subseteq\Xi
.
Then the desired preproof is

\beta(w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi},\pi_{0})

(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow

{\neg}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow\neg(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i}

\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\neg(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*},\Lambda

{\neg}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\scalebox{1.1}{$\meddiamond$}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4}}_{i})
and the principal formula is in
\Lambda
.
Then
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{\prime}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{\prime}_{\overline{i}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow\phi

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi,\Lambda^{\prime}
,
\Sigma^{\prime}_{i}\subseteq\Sigma_{i}
,
\Sigma^{\prime}_{\overline{i}}\subseteq\Sigma_{\overline{i}}
and
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\subseteq\Xi
.
Then the desired preproof is

\beta(w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}},\pi_{0})

(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow\phi

\mathrm{Wk}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Xi_{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}}\Rightarrow\phi

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*},\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi,\Lambda^{\prime}

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi,\Lambda^{\prime}

Finally, let us argue that
\beta(w,\pi)
is always a proof and not only a preproof.
Given a node
w
assign to it the measure
\omega^{2}|\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow\Delta_{w}|+\omega\ell(w)+\mathrm{lhg}(\pi)
where
\ell(w)
is the length of
w
as a sequence of natural numbers.
We notice that this measure always decreases from
\beta(w,\pi)
to its corecursive calls, except when
w
is annotated with the rule
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{+})
and the last rule of
\pi
is
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{CS}}_{i}
.
However, in this case we will find that progress is made (i.e., there is an application of
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i})
) from the root of the preproof fragment given by
\beta(w,\pi)
to the corecursive calls.
This implies that any infinite branch will have infinitely many applications of
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{i})
, as otherwise we will infinitely decrease the measure on corecursive calls.
∎

After all the necessary technical work, we can finally conclude the desired result.

5.3
.

\mathsf{CS}
has uniform Lyndon interpolation.

Proof.

Let
T
be an interpolation template for
\phi\Rightarrow
.
We already know that
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\iota_{T})\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
and by Theorem
5.1
we have that
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi\to\iota_{T}
.
Finally, assume that
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi\to\psi
where
\psi
is such that
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\psi)\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.
Then
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi\Rightarrow\psi
and by Theorem
5.2
we obtain that
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{CS}\vdash\iota_{T}\Rightarrow\psi
, i.e.,
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\iota_{T}\to\psi
as desired.
∎

Using the uniform Lyndon interpolation of
\mathsf{CS}
we show it also for
\mathsf{CSM}
.
First let us define a function
(\cdot)^{\sharp}:\mathrm{Fm}\xlongrightarrow{}\mathrm{Fm}
as follows:

\displaystyle p^{\sharp}=p,

\displaystyle\bot^{\sharp}=\bot,

\displaystyle(\phi\to\psi)^{\sharp}=\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp},

\displaystyle(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp},

\displaystyle(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}.

The following lemma shows that
(\cdot)^{\sharp}
is an interpretation of
\mathsf{CSM}
in
\mathsf{CS}
.

{lemma}

Let
\phi
be any formula, we have the following.

1.

\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\phi^{\sharp})\subseteq\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\phi)
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.

2.

\mathsf{CSM}\vdash\phi\leftrightarrow\phi^{\sharp}
.

3.

If
\mathsf{CSM}\vdash\phi
then
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi^{\sharp}
.

Proof.

The first two points follow easily by induction on the complexity of the formula
\phi
.
To show the last point it suffices to prove that
\left\{\phi\mid\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi^{\sharp}\right\}
contains the axioms of
\mathsf{CSM}
and is closed under
(\mathrm{MP})
and
(\mathrm{Nec}_{i})
for
i\leq 1
.
The closure properties are straightforward to show, so we focus in showing that all the axioms of
\mathsf{CS}
belong to that set.
For
(\mathrm{K}_{1})
and
(\mathrm{L}_{1})
the proof is trivial, as
(\cdot)^{\sharp}
commutes will all the connectives occuring in those axiom schemes.

Axiom
(\mathrm{K}_{0})
.
We have that

(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\phi\to\psi)\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\psi)^{\sharp}=(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp}))\to(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp})\to(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\psi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\psi^{\sharp}).

That
\mathsf{CS}
proves that formula is equivalent to

\mathsf{CS}\vdash\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\psi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\psi^{\sharp},

which clearly holds by
(\mathrm{K}_{0})
and
(\mathrm{K}_{1})
.

Axiom
(\mathrm{L}_{0})
.
We have that

(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi\to\phi)\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}.

Proving this in
\mathsf{CS}
is equivalent to showing

\mathsf{CS}\vdash\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}.

We show the proof of this formula in
\mathcal{G}\mathsf{CS}
.
Let
\Gamma=\left\{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\to\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\right\}
.
Define
\pi_{i}
for
i\leq 1
to be the proof

\mathrm{Ax}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\phi^{\sharp}

\mathrm{Ax}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\phi^{\sharp}

{\wedge}\mathrm{R}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\phi^{\sharp}

\mathrm{Ax}

\phi^{\sharp},\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp}

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp},\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{CS}}_{\overline{i}}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}

Then define
\tau_{i}
for
i\leq 1
to be the proof

\mathrm{Ax}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}

\pi_{i}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}

{\wedge}\mathrm{R}

\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}

\mathrm{Ax}

\phi^{\sharp},\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp}

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp},\Gamma,\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}\Rightarrow\phi^{\sharp}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{CS}}_{i}

\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\phi^{\sharp}

Then the desired proof is

\tau_{0}

\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}

\tau_{1}

\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}

{\wedge}\mathrm{R}

\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}

{\to}\mathrm{R}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\to\phi^{\sharp})\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}

Axiom
(\mathrm{C}_{1,0})
.
We have that
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp})
.
By normality, showing that
\mathsf{CS}
proves this is equivalent to showing that

\mathsf{CS}\vdash\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp},

which clearly holds using
(\mathrm{C}_{1,0})
and transitivity of
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}
.

Axiom
(\mathrm{C}_{0,1})
.
We have that
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}
.
Again,
\mathsf{CS}
clearly proves this using
\mathrm{C}_{1,0}
and transitivity of
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}
.

Axiom
(\mathrm{M}_{0,1})
.
We have that
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi)^{\sharp}=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi^{\sharp}\wedge\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}\to\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\phi^{\sharp}
.
Clearly,
\mathsf{CS}
proves this as it is a propositional tautology.
∎

We can finally conclude that
\mathsf{CSM}
has uniform Lyndon interpolation.

5.4
.

\mathsf{CSM}
has uniform Lyndon interpolation.

Proof.

Let
\phi
be a formula and
V_{+}
and
V_{-}
be vocabularies.
Note that
\phi^{\sharp}
has a
\mathsf{CS}
-uniform Lyndon interpolant
\iota
. Let us show that
\iota
is the
\mathsf{CSM}
-uniform Lyndon interpolant of
\phi
.
We automatically have that
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\iota)\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.
From
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi^{\sharp}\to\iota
we obtain that
\mathsf{CSM}\vdash\phi\to\iota
using that
\mathsf{CS}\subseteq\mathsf{CSM}
and Lemma
5.1
.

Finally, assume that
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi\to\psi
where
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\psi)\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.
By Lemma
5.1
we have that
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\phi^{\sharp}\to\psi^{\sharp}
and
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\psi^{\sharp})\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.
Using that
\iota
is the
\mathsf{CS}
-uniform Lyndon interpolant of
\phi^{\sharp}
we obtain that
\mathsf{CS}\vdash\iota\to\psi^{\sharp}
.
Finally, using Lemma
5.1
and that
\mathsf{CS}\subseteq\mathsf{CSM}
we obtain that
\mathsf{CSM}\vdash\iota\to\psi
, as desired.
∎

5.2
Uniform Lyndon Interpolation for ER

Contrary to the
\mathsf{CS}
case, the rules of
\mathsf{ER}
do not always lower the complexity of sequents, due to the rule
(\mathrm{ER}_{1,0})
.
For this reason, we will need to change a bit the proofs we did for
\mathsf{CS}
in the previous subsection.
The main difference is that we will need a notion of saturation (defined below) to exhaust the proof search space.

{definition}

Let
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
be an
\mathsf{ER}
-sequent.
We say that a formula
\phi
is

1.

Left-saturated
if

(a)

\phi
is atomic or a
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}
-formula,

(b)

\phi=\phi_{0}\to\phi_{1}
and
\phi_{0}\in\Delta
or
\phi_{1}\in\Gamma
,

(c)

\phi=\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi_{0}
and
i=0
or
i=1
and
\phi_{0}\in\Gamma
.

2.

Right-saturated
if

(a)

\phi
is atomic or a
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}
-formula for
i\leq 1
.

(b)

\phi=\phi_{0}\to\phi_{1}
and
\phi_{0}\in\Gamma
and
\phi_{1}\in\Delta
,

A sequent
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
is said to be
saturated
if every
\phi\in\Gamma
is left-saturated in
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
and every
\psi\in\Delta
is right-saturated in
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
.
We define the
saturation complexity
of a sequent
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
, denoted
|\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta|_{\mathrm{Sat}}
, as the number

\sum\left(\left\{|\phi|\mid\phi\in\Gamma,\text{$\phi$ not left-saturated in $\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta$}\right\}\cup\left\{|\phi|\mid\phi\in\Delta,\text{$\phi$ not right-saturated in $\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta$}\right\}\right).

Notice that if
(S_{0},\ldots,S_{n-1},S)
is an instance of a rule for interpolation templates distinct from
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}
then
|S_{i}|_{\mathrm{Sat}}<|S|_{\mathrm{Sat}}
for
i<n
.

\mathrm{Emp}

\Rightarrow

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{1}\Delta

\mathrm{ER}_{1,0}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{1}\Delta

where in
(\mathrm{ER}_{1,0}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
the formula
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi
is not left-saturated at the conclusion.

\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi,\Delta

\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta

{\to}\mathrm{L}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta

\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Delta

{\to}\mathrm{R}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi\to\psi,\Delta

where

1.

in
({\to}\mathrm{L}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
the formula
\phi\to\psi
is not left-saturated at the conclusion,

2.

in
({\to}\mathrm{R}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
the formula
\phi\to\psi
is not right-saturated at the conclusion.

[\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow_{i}]_{i\leq 1}

[\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow_{i}\phi]_{i\leq 1,\phi\in\Theta_{i}}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta

where

1.

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta
is saturated,

2.

\Gamma,\Delta
contain no
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits
-formulas,

3.

(\Gamma\cap\Delta)\cap\text{Var}=\varnothing
and
\bot\not\ \in\Gamma
(in other words, the conclusion is not an instance of
(\mathrm{ax})
or
({\bot}\mathrm{L})
).

Figure 10:
Interpolation template rules for
\mathsf{ER}

{definition}

An
\mathsf{ER}
-interpolation template
is a cyclic proof in the local progress calculus with the rules
(\mathrm{ax}),({\bot}\mathrm{L})
from Figure
5
and the rules of Figure
10
, where progress is only made at the premises of
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}
.

Mimicking the proof of Lemma
5.1
, changing the measure on sequents from
|S|
to
|S|_{\mathrm{Sat}}
, we obtain the following lemma.

{lemma}

Every
\mathsf{ER}
-sequent has an
\mathsf{ER}
-interpolation template.

We fix vocabularies
V_{+}
and
V_{-}
for which we want to calculate the uniform Lyndon interpolant.

{definition}

Let
T
be an interpolation template and
w
a node of
T
, let us denote the sequent at
w
as
\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow\Delta_{w}
.
We will annotate each of the sequents of
T
with a formula
\kappa
called the
preinterpolant at
\kappa
, denoted as
\kappa:\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow\Delta_{w}
.
We proceed by induction on the tree structure of
T
.
If
w
is a repeat node of shape

\mathrm{Rep}

\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w}

then
\kappa_{w}
will be a fresh propositional variable
x_{w}
.
This variable shall not appear in any formula of
T
and for repeat nodes
w,v
such that
w\neq v
we must have that
x_{w}\neq x_{v}
.
In case
w
is not a repeat node we proceed by cases on the rule at
w
, we describe the construction pictorically as follows (the preinterpolant at the conclusion is defined recursively from the preinterpolants at the premises):

\mathrm{ax}

\bot:p,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}p,\Delta

{\bot}\mathrm{L}

\bot:\bot,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta

\mathrm{Emp}

\top:{\Rightarrow_{i}}

\kappa_{0}:\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi,\Delta

\kappa_{1}:\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta

{\to}\mathrm{L}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\kappa_{0}\vee\kappa_{1}:\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta

\kappa:\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Delta

{\to}\mathrm{R}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\kappa:\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi\to\psi,\Delta

\kappa:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{1}\Delta

\mathrm{ER}_{1,0}^{\mathrm{Sat}}

\kappa:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{1}\Delta

[\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow_{i}]_{i\leq 1}

[\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow_{i}\phi]_{i\leq 1,\phi\in\Theta_{i}}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}

\kappa:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta

where

\kappa:=\bigwedge_{i\leq 1}\left(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}\wedge\bigwedge_{\phi\in\Theta_{i}}\mathop{\text{\scalebox{1.1}{$\meddiamond$}}}\nolimits_{i}\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}\right)\wedge\bigwedge(\Gamma\cap V_{+})\wedge\bigwedge\neg(\Delta\cap V_{-}).

Again, each interpolation template gives an equational system that we proceed to define. For each
i\in\mathbb{N}
let
\bar{x}_{i}
be an arbitrary enumeration of
\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T),\mathrm{hg}(w^{\circ})=i\right\}
.
If
\mathrm{hg}(T)=H
, then we define
\bar{x}_{T}=\bar{x}_{0}\bar{x}_{1}\cdots\bar{x}_{H}
,
\bar{x}_{T}
is an anumeration of
\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}
.
We define the
equational system of
T
, denoted
\mathcal{E}_{T}
, as

\left\{(x_{w},+,\kappa_{w})\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}.

In the following lemma we show that it
\mathcal{E}_{T}
is solvable in
\mathsf{ER}
.

{lemma}

\mathcal{E}_{T}
is a positive modalized Lyndon equational system over
(\bar{x}_{T},V_{+},V_{-})
.
As a corollary,
\mathcal{E}_{T}
has a solution in
\mathsf{CS}
.

Proof.

The proof follows the same structure as the proof of Lemma
5.1
.
∎

The definition of interpolant is analogous to the
\mathsf{CS}
logic using interpolation templates and equational systems.
Notice that the same remarks about the uniqueness of interpolants that we made for
\mathsf{CS}
can be applied to
ER
(see page
5.1
).

{definition}

[Interpolant]
Given an interpolation template
T
we define the interpolant of
T
, denoted
\iota_{T}
, as the formula obtained by applying the substitution solving
\mathcal{E}_{T}
to the preinterpolant at the root of
T
.

As in the case of
\mathsf{CS}
, we finish by proving that the intepolant has the necessary properties.
Again, showing that
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\iota_{T})\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
is straightforward by definition (see page
5.1
).
The proofs of the two following theorems differ slightly at some points from those for
\mathsf{CS}
.
In particular, we have to exploit the properties of saturation, as now we measure the complexity of sequent via saturation complexity.

5.5
.

Let
T
be an interpolation template for
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
.
Then
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}\vdash\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta,\iota_{T}
.

Proof.

Given a node
w
of
T
let us denote the sequent at
w
as
\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w}
, the preinterpolant of
T
at
w
as
\kappa_{w}
and assume
(\cdot)^{*}
is a solution of
\mathcal{E}_{T}
.
We define a function
\alpha
such that if
w
is a node of
T
then
\alpha(w)
is a preproof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}+\mathrm{Cut}+\mathrm{Wk}
of
\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w},\kappa^{*}_{w}
.
Then we will show that
\alpha(w)
is in fact a proof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}+\mathrm{Cut}+\mathrm{Wk}
and then using that
\mathrm{Wk}
is eliminable in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}+\mathrm{Cut}
and that
\mathrm{Cut}
is eliminable in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
we obtain the desired proof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
.

We proceed to define
\alpha
by corecursion and cases.
First, assume
w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)
then
w^{\circ}
has the same sequent as
w
and
\kappa_{w}=x_{w}
.
Also, by virtue of
(\cdot)^{*}
being a solution of
\mathcal{E}_{T}
, we have that
\mathsf{ER}\vdash x^{*}_{w}\leftrightarrow\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}}
.
Then we define
\alpha
as

where the proof
\tau
exists since
\mathsf{ER}\vdash x^{*}_{w}\leftrightarrow\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}}
(so also
\mathsf{ER}\vdash\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}(x^{*}_{w}\leftrightarrow\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}})
).

Now assume
w
is not a repetition node and let
R
be the rule at
w
.
Below there are the definitions when
R
is
(\mathrm{ax})
,
(\mathrm{Emp})
,
({\bot}\mathrm{L})
,
({\to}\mathrm{L}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
,
({\to}\mathrm{R}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
or
(\mathrm{ER}_{1,0}^{\mathrm{Sat}})
.

Finally, let us define
\alpha(w)
when

Then
w
is of shape

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}\\

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}\\

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}

\kappa_{w}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta

where

\kappa^{*}_{w}:=\bigwedge_{i\leq 1}\left(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*}\wedge\bigwedge\limits_{\phi\in\Theta_{i}}\mathop{\text{\scalebox{1.1}{$\meddiamond$}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*}\right)\wedge\bigwedge(\Gamma\cap V_{+})\wedge\bigwedge\neg(\Delta\cap V_{-})

Since
\kappa^{*}_{w}
is a conjunction of formulas it suffices to construct a preproof for each conjunct and join them with
({\wedge}\mathrm{R})
.

Finally, let us argue that
\alpha(w)
is always a proof and not only a preproof.
Given a node
w
assign to it the measure
\omega|\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w}|_{\mathrm{Sat}}+\ell(w)
where
\ell(w)
is the length of
w
as a sequence of natural numbers.
We notice that this measure always decreases from
\alpha(w)
to its corecursive call, except when
w
is annotated with the rule
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+})
.
However, in this case we will find that progress is made (i.e., there is an application of
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{i})
) from the root of the preproof fragment given by
\alpha(w)
to the corecursive calls.
This implies that any infinite branch will have infinitely many applications of
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{i})
, as otherwise we will infinitely decrease the measure on corecursive calls.
∎

5.6
.

Let
T
be an interpolation template for
\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta
and
\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda
be a sequent such that
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda)\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
.
Then
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}\vdash\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta,\Lambda
implies
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}\vdash\iota_{T},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda
.

Proof.

Given a node
w
of
T
let us denote the sequent at
w
as
\Gamma_{w}\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w},
, the preinterpolant at
w
as
\kappa_{w}
and assume
(\cdot)^{*}
is a solution of
\mathcal{E}_{T}
.
We define a function
\beta
such that if
w
is a node of
T
and
\pi
is a proof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
of
\Gamma_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Delta_{w},\Lambda
where
\Xi\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Lambda
is a sequent with
\mathrm{Voc}_{b}(\Xi\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Lambda)\subseteq V_{b}
for
b\in\left\{+,-\right\}
, then
\beta(w,\pi)
is a preproof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}+\mathrm{Cut}+\mathrm{Wk}
of
\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i_{w}}\Lambda
.
Then we will show that
\beta(w,\pi)
is in fact a proof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}+\mathrm{Cut}+\mathrm{Wk}
and then using that
\mathrm{Wk}
is eliminable in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
and that
\mathrm{Cut}
is eliminable in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
we obtian the desired proof in
\mathcal{G}^{\infty}\mathsf{ER}
.

We proceed to define
\beta
by corecursion and cases.
First, assume
w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)
then
w^{\circ}
has the same sequent as
w
and
\kappa_{w}=x_{w}
.
Also, by virtue of
(\cdot)^{*}
being a solution of
\mathcal{E}_{T}
, we have that
\mathsf{ER}\vdash x^{*}_{w}\leftrightarrow\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}}
.
Then we define
\beta
as

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.11299pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.21727pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip-1.88701pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to56.43456pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 1.94257pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Rep}$}}\vskip-1.88701pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$x_{w}:\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces,\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 23.74504pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 30.86485pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.41985pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\tau$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$x^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda,\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}}$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}\hbox{\hskip 14.45377pt}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 8.31654pt\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.48695pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w^{\circ},\pi)$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to75.21121pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w^{\circ}},x^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip where the proof

exists since

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 18.51796pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.9701pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{0}:\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}\hbox{\hskip 14.45377pt}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 36.24788pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w1$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{1}:\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-1.70427pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to199.74625pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 3.70425pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\to}\mathrm{L}^{\mathrm{Sat}}$}}\vskip-1.70425pt\hbox{\hskip 49.32199pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{0}\vee\kappa_{1}:\phi\to\psi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.4811pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\phi\to\psi,\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\\
\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.8352pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w0,\mathrm{Wk}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 6.5762pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{0},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\hbox{\hskip 14.45377pt}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w1,\mathrm{Wk}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 6.5762pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{1},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\vskip-0.9148pt\hbox{\hskip 6.5762pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to132.64891pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.91478pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\vee}\mathrm{L}$}}\vskip-0.91478pt\hbox{\hskip 37.01614pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{0}\vee\kappa^{*}_{1},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 18.51796pt\hbox{\vbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 42.4429pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\phi,\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\psi,\phi\to\psi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}\vskip-1.70427pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to105.31406pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 3.70425pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt${\to}\mathrm{R}^{\mathrm{Sat}}$}}\vskip-1.70425pt\hbox{\hskip 10.68054pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa_{w}:\Gamma\Rightarrow_{i}\phi\to\psi,\Delta$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces,\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 7.15277pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.4811pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\pi$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\phi\to\psi,\Delta,\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\ignorespaces\right)\quad\overset{\beta}{\longmapsto}\quad\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 19.77965pt\hbox{\vbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\beta(w0,\mathrm{Wk}(\pi))$}\hskip 4.0pt}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 5.63812pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 5.63812pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to54.39754pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 5.63812pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

\left(\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower 17.62703pt\hbox{\vbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 33.0738pt\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$w0$}\hskip 4.0pt}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to86.57587pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.27371pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{ER}_{1,0}^{\mathrm{Sat}}$}}\vskip-3.13481pt\hbox{\hskip 0.25777pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{}\hbox{\vbox{\vskip 1.0pt}}\lower 0.0pt\hbox{}}\vskip 2.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 5.63812pt\lower-0.50186pt\hbox{}\hbox to54.39754pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}}\lower 2.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 3.0pt$\mathrm{Wk}$}}\vskip-0.97035pt\hbox{\hskip 5.63812pt\hbox{\hbox{\hskip 4.0pt\hbox{$\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow_{i}\Lambda$}\hskip 4.0pt}}}}}\ignorespaces

Finally, assume
R
is
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4CS}}_{+})
.
Then
w
is of shape

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}\\
\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s}_{i},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{\overline{i}}\Rightarrow_{i}\end{matrix}\right]_{i\leq 1}

\left[\begin{matrix}w^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}\\
\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquaredot$}}}}\nolimits_{i}\Sigma^{s},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{\overline{i}}\Sigma^{s}_{i}\Rightarrow_{i}\phi\end{matrix}\right]_{i\leq 1,\phi\in\Theta_{i}}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}

\kappa_{w}:\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta

where

\kappa^{*}_{w}:=\bigwedge_{i\leq 1}\left(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}})^{*}\wedge\bigwedge_{\phi\in\Theta_{i}}\mathop{\text{\scalebox{1.1}{$\meddiamond$}}}\nolimits_{i}(\kappa^{\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.3014pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{i}}_{\phi})^{*}\right)\wedge\bigwedge(\Gamma\cap V_{+})\wedge\bigwedge\neg(\Delta\cap V_{-}).

We define
\beta(w,\pi)
by case analysis on the shape of
\pi
.

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
(\mathrm{ax})
.
There are
4
cases depending on where the repeated propositional variable is located.

–

Assume
p\in\Gamma\cap\in\Delta
.
This case is impossible, as
w
is annotated with
(\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+})
so
(\Gamma\cap\Delta)\cap\text{Var}=\varnothing
.

–

Assume
p\in\Gamma\cap\Lambda
.
Since
p\in\Lambda
and
\mathrm{Voc}_{+}(\Xi\gg\Lambda)\subseteq V_{+}
we have that
p\in V_{+}
.
Then as
p\in\Gamma\cap V_{+}
and
p\not\in\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}
, we obtain that
p
is a conjunct of
\kappa^{*}_{w}
.
The desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

p,\Xi\gg p,\Lambda^{\prime}

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg p,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=p,\Lambda^{\prime}
.

–

Assume
p\in\Xi\cap\Delta
.
Since
p\in\Xi
and
\mathrm{Voc}_{-}(\Xi\gg\Lambda)\subseteq V_{-}
we have that
p\in V_{-}
.
Then as
p\in\Delta\cap V_{-}
and
p\not\in\left\{x_{w}\mid w\in\mathrm{Rep}(T)\right\}
, we obtain that
\neg p
is a conjunct of
\kappa^{*}_{w}
.
The desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

p,\Xi^{\prime}\gg p,\Lambda

{\neg}\mathrm{L}

\neg p,p,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}+{\wedge}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},p,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\Lambda

where
\Xi=p,\Xi^{\prime}
.

–

Assume
p\in\Xi\cap\Lambda
.
Then the desired preproof is

\mathrm{ax}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\bot}\mathrm{L})
.
Since
\bot
is not in
\Gamma
, then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

{\bot}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\bot,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Xi=\bot,\Xi^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

{\bot}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\bot,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\bot}\mathrm{R})
and principal formula is in
\Delta
.
Then
\pi
is of shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta^{\prime},\Lambda

{\bot}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\bot,\Delta^{\prime},\Lambda

where
\Delta=\bot,\Delta^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\mathrm{Wk}(\pi_{0}))

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\bot}\mathrm{R})
and principal formula is in
\Lambda
.
Then
\pi
is of shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\bot}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\Rightarrow\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\Lambda^{\prime}

{\bot}\mathrm{R}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\Rightarrow\bot,\Lambda^{\prime}

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{L})
and principal formula is in
\Gamma
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma^{\prime},\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\phi,\Delta,\Lambda

\pi_{1}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\psi,\Gamma^{\prime},\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\phi\to\psi,\Gamma^{\prime},\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Gamma=\phi\to\psi,\Gamma^{\prime}
.
By saturation (the conclusion of
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}
is saturated) we have that either
\phi\in\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta
or
\psi\in\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma^{\prime}
.
In the first case,
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\mathrm{Wk}(\mathrm{Ctr}(\pi_{0})))

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\Lambda

where in
\pi_{0}
first we contract
\phi
on the right and then
\phi\to\psi
is reintroduced via weakening.
In the second case
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined analogously using
\pi_{1}
instead of
\pi_{0}
.

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{L})
and principal formula is in
\Xi
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\phi,\Lambda

\pi_{1}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\Lambda

where
\Xi=\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi^{\prime}\gg\phi,\Lambda

\beta(w,\pi_{1})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\phi\to\psi,\Xi^{\prime}\gg\Lambda

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{R})
and principal formula is in
\Delta
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\phi,\Gamma,\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\psi,\Delta^{\prime},\Lambda

{\to}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\phi\to\psi,\Delta^{\prime},\Lambda

where
\Delta=\phi\to\psi,\Delta^{\prime}
.
By saturation (the conclusion of
\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits^{\mathsf{K4ER}}_{+}
is saturated) we have that
\phi\in\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma
and
\psi\in\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\mathrm{Wk}(\mathrm{Ctr}(\pi_{0})))

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\Lambda

\mathrm{Wk}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\Lambda

where in
\pi_{0}
first we contract
\phi
on the left and
\psi
on the right and then
\phi\to\psi
is reintroduced via weakening.

•

Last rule of
\pi
is
({\to}\mathrm{R})
and principal formula is in
\Lambda
.
Then we have that
\pi
has the following shape

\pi_{0}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\phi,\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\to}\mathrm{R}

\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Sigma_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Sigma_{1},\Gamma,\Xi\gg\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{0}\Theta_{0},\mathop{\text{\raisebox{-0.43057pt}{\scalebox{1.3}{$\medsquare$}}}}\nolimits_{1}\Theta_{1},\Delta,\phi\to\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

where
\Lambda=\phi\to\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}
.
Then
\beta(w,\pi)
is defined as

\beta(w,\pi_{0})

\kappa^{*}_{w},\phi,\Xi\gg\psi,\Lambda^{\prime}

{\to}\mathrm{L}

\kappa^{*}_{w},\Xi\gg\phi\to\psi,\Lambda

\pi
is
(\mathrm{ER}_{1,0})

.
Note that in this case

.

\beta(w,\pi)

\mathrm{Wk}
